Structural cue: a raised cloud from earth appears before the explicit eschatological upturning in 100:9. Role: dust is a preliminary sign of the ground's contents being disturbed. Abductive move: military dust becomes an image-scale rehearsal for the later cosmic excavation. Reading change: 100:4 is not only battlefield atmosphere; it anticipates earth giving up what was settled within it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001544/B001","root_001544/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_24f359c99e9f76d212ac","text":"2. **A dormant basin image sharpens what is disturbed.** `ن ق ع B001` is settled water or material left steeping, and `B007` is level clay ground, while `ث و ر B001` is something bursting outward after stillness or concealment. These branches make the raised `نَقْعًا` the reversal of settling: a receptive surface is made to throw up what it held. The packet supplies the opposed states, but the shared state-transition model across water, clay, and dust is my analogy. The dust is thus read as the ground's former deposit forcibly mobilized, not as an unrelated atmospheric decoration.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000210/B002","root_001195/B002","root_001544/B004","root_001642/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2c77450c282907318626","text":"1. **Dust as displaced concealment.** Evidence: ث و ر B002 stirring something from settled state; ن ق ع B004 raised dust; و ر ي B005 covering/hiding; ق ب ر B002 hidden inward sinking. Structural cue: the sequence moves from hidden fire to raised dust, then later to opened graves. Role: ثور supplies the act of agitation; نقع supplies the visible medium lifted from the ground. Abductive move: the same force that exposes sparks also produces a screen of dust, so disclosure and obscuration co-occur. Reading change: the ayah reads as a paradoxical unveiling that also veils.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001544/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_698efc700a3bc3183ee2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:4:3\",\"root_001544\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Toz bulutu, soluk ve sabah baskınıyla birleşerek yalnız maddeyi kaldırmaz, topluluğu da alarma geçirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000210/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_890081850826f56197e6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:4:1\",\"root_000210\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Toprağın durgun içeriğinin yukarı fırlaması, mezarlardakilerin ileride dışarı çıkarılmasının küçük ölçekli bir provasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000130/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_91ddf8c5cdafb151cb2d","text":"100:9 is the decisive surprise. `ب ع ث ر B001` repeats the same earth-disturbing operation at the scale of buried contents, while `ع ل م B001` makes the result epistemic. This corrects an exclusively obscuring reading of the dust: locally the cloud can screen, yet structurally its stirring already prototypes the later removal of concealment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000210/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9a2ed013d1bc33317114","text":"1. **Settled ground converted into an airborne screen.** `ث و ر B002` supplies stirring earth or dust from a settled state, and `ن ق ع B004` supplies raised dust. The causative verb and its material result follow the run, strike, and raid. Disturbance is the operation; suspension and spread are the result. The packet gives that sequence, while I infer that the preceding impacts provide the energy of displacement. The ayah reads as a precise change of material state, from settled surface to mobile cloud.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000210/B002","root_001544/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a69f74a29b4b1bf495a5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:4:1\",\"root_000210\",[\"B002\"]],[\"100:4:3\",\"root_001544\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yerden kaldırılan toz, saklı olanı hareket ettirirken aynı anda görüşü örten paradoksal bir açığa çıkarma yaratır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000210/B001","root_001544/B001","root_001544/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b8dd16daa5e2e473d5e5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:4:1\",\"root_000210\",[\"B001\"]],[\"100:4:3\",\"root_001544\",[\"B001\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yerleşmiş su veya düz kil zemini çağrışımı, tozu zeminin tuttuğu birikimin zorla havaya kaldırılması olarak gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000839/B004","root_001544/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_cad0e5a6fa7c9a1837aa","text":"3. **Noise becoming social alarm.** Evidence: ن ق ع B005 raised/sustained cry; ض ب ح B001 breath-sound; ص ب ح B004 morning alarm; ش ه د B008 visible sign. Structural cue: sound begins with animal breath, then the raid at morning implies alarm, then dust becomes a visible sign. Role: نقع as cry converts particulate disturbance into communicative disturbance. Abductive move: the dust cloud and sound together warn the gathered group before impact. Reading change: the ayah becomes an alarm event: the raid raises both matter and public awareness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000130/B001","root_000210/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d79da4b0e29ab7397034","text":"3. **Physical stirring becomes a model of epistemic excavation.** `ث و ر B002` explicitly includes searching out hidden knowledge by stirring what is concealed. The fixed dust-raising and later grave-opening share disturbance of a covering medium.
